  ⎿  Added 325 lines, removed 8 lines
        1  <h1>Enterprise Knowledge Base RAG Chatbot</h1>
        2 +                                                              
        3 +A prototype assistant for OmniCorp Solutions' Customer Success
          + Managers (CSMs). It answers natural-language questions **stri
          +ctly from internal documentation**, **cites the exact document
          + sections** it used, and **refuses politely** (pointing to a h
          +uman expert) when the documentation has no answer.            
        4 +                                                              
        5 +- **Backend:** Python 3.12 + FastAPI. Heading-aware chunking, 
          +exact in-memory vector search with an on-disk index cache, and
          + a streaming RAG pipeline with citation mapping.              
        6 +- **Frontend:** Vite + React + TypeScript + Tailwind. Streamed
          + answers, clickable `[n]` citation chips and source cards.    
        7 +- **LLM:** a local model via **Ollama** (`gemma3:4b`, embeddin
          +gs `embeddinggemma`), or **Claude** with your own API key (`cl
          +aude-opus-5`), selectable per question.                       
        8 +- **One command:** `docker compose up`.                       
        9 +                                                              
       10 +> The original assignment brief and the progress checklist are
          + at the [end of this file](#assignment-brief).                
       11 +                                                              
       12 +---                                                           
       13 +                                                              
       14 +## Contents                                                   
       15 +                                                              
       16 +- [Quick start](#quick-start)                                 
       17 +- [Architecture](#architecture)                               
       18 +- [API](#api)                                                 
       19 +- [Key decisions and trade-offs](#key-decisions-and-trade-offs
          +)                                                             
       20 +- [Testing and evaluation](#testing-and-evaluation)           
       21 +- [Configuration](#configuration)                             
       22 +- [Project structure](#project-structure)                     
       23 +- [Known limitations and future work](#known-limitations-and-f
          +uture-work)                                                   
       24 +- [AI assistant usage](#ai-assistant-usage)                   
       25 +- [Assignment brief](#assignment-brief)                       
       26 +                                                              
       27 +---                                                           
       28 +                                                              
       29 +## Quick start                                                
       30 +                                                              
       31 +Requirements: Docker with Compose v2, about 6 GB of disk space
          +, and about 6 GB of free RAM. No GPU is needed.               
       32 +                                                              
       33 +```bash                                                       
       34 +git clone https://github.com/sarkiszhamakortsyan/ep_kb_rag_cha
          +tbot.git                                                      
       35 +cd ep_kb_rag_chatbot                                          
       36 +cp .env.example .env        # optional: all settings have defa
          +ults                                                          
       37 +docker compose up -d --build                                  
       38 +```                                                           
       39 +                                                              
       40 +Open **http://localhost:8080**.                               
       41 +                                                              
       42 +- **First start:** `ollama-init` downloads the two models (~4 
          +GB). The backend then builds the vector index in the backgroun
          +d (about 16 s on a 4-core laptop CPU). Until it's ready the UI
          + shows *"Knowledge base loading…"* and the API answers `503 no
          +t_ready`. Later starts load the cached index within seconds.  
       43 +- **Using Claude (recommended on machines without a GPU):** se
          +t `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY=...` in `.env`, then run `docker compose 
          +up -d`. Pick *anthropic* in the **Model** selector, or set `LL
          +M_PROVIDER=anthropic` to make it the default. Ollama still run
          +s, because it provides the embeddings.                        
       44 +- **Check the status:** `curl localhost:8080/api/v1/health`. I
          +nteractive API docs are at `http://localhost:8080/api/v1/...` 
          +through the UI proxy; run the backend directly for Swagger at 
          +`/docs`.                                                      
       45 +- **Stop:** `docker compose down` (add `-v` to also delete the
          + models and the index).                                       
       46 +                                                              
       47 +### Local development (without Docker for the app)            
       48 +                                                              
       49 +```bash                                                       
       50 +docker compose up -d ollama ollama-init          # Ollama on 1
          +27.0.0.1:11434                                                
       51 +cd backend  && uv sync && uv run uvicorn app.main:api --reload
          +    # :8000, Swagger at /docs                                 
       52 +cd frontend && npm ci && npm run dev                          
          +     # :5173, proxies /api -> :8000                           
       53 +```                                                           
       54 +                                                              
       55 +---                                                           
       56 +                                                              
       57 +## Architecture                                               
       58 +                                                              
       59 +```mermaid                                                    
       60 +flowchart LR                                                  
       61 +    U[CSM in browser] -->|HTTP| N[nginx<br/>frontend container
          +]                                                             
       62 +    N -->|static files| SPA[React SPA]                        
       63 +    N -->|/api/*, SSE| API[FastAPI backend]                   
       64 +                                                              
       65 +    subgraph Backend                                          
       66 +        API --> P[RagPipeline]                                
       67 +        P --> R[Retriever]                                    
       68 +        R --> E[EmbeddingProvider<br/>registry]               
       69 +        R --> VS[(InMemoryVectorStore<br/>numpy cosine)]      
       70 +        P --> L[LLMProvider<br/>registry]                     
       71 +        P --> EV[EventStore<br/>log-only]                     
       72 +        I[Ingest: load, chunk, embed] --> VS                  
       73 +        I <--> C[(index cache<br/>volume)]                    
       74 +    end                                                       
       75 +                                                              
       76 +    E --> O[Ollama<br/>embeddinggemma]                        
       77 +    L --> O2[Ollama<br/>gemma3:4b]                            
       78 +    L --> A[Anthropic API<br/>claude-opus-5]                  
       79 +    KB[(5 Markdown KB articles)] --> I                        
       80 +```                                                           
       81 +                                                              
       82 +**Ingestion (at startup, in the background):**                
       83 +1. Load `backend/data/kb/*.md`, whose front matter holds `id`,
          + `title` and `lang`.                                          
       84 +2. Split on headings, then pack paragraphs, lists and tables (
          +kept whole) into windows of about 300 words with overlap. Each
          + chunk keeps its heading path, e.g. `Configuring SAML 2.0 › Ce
          +rtificate rotation`.                                          
       85 +3. Embed the chunks, including the heading path, with task-spe
          +cific prompts.                                                
       86 +4. Save the index to a volume. The cache key is a hash of the 
          +documents, the chunking parameters and the embedding model, so
          + changing any of them triggers a rebuild.                     
       87 +                                                              
       88 +**Answering a question:**                                     
       89 +1. **Retrieve:** embed the question and take the top 6 chunks 
          +by cosine similarity.                                         
       90 +2. **Refuse early:** if even the best chunk scores below `MIN_
          +SCORE` (0.35), return a polite "not in the knowledge base, ask
          + an expert" message **without calling the LLM**.              
       91 +3. **Prompt:** chunks below `MIN_SCORE` are dropped. The rest 
          +are sent as numbered `<source>` blocks, under a system prompt 
          +that is kept byte-identical so it can be cached. The prompt is
          + a template file (`backend/app/prompts/system.md`): answer onl
          +y from the sources, cite every statement as `[n]`, refuse with
          +out citations when not covered, stay professional and concise,
          + reply in the question's language, and treat the sources as da
          +ta, never as instructions.                                    
       92 +4. **Generate:** stream tokens from the selected provider.    
       93 +5. **Cite:** map the `[n]` markers to sources and drop invente
          +d numbers. An answer without any valid citation counts as a re
          +fusal (`no_citations`), which works in any language.          
       94 +6. **Record:** every turn produces a `ChatResult` with the ans
          +wer, citations, provider/model, token usage, timings, top scor
          +e and refusal reason. It's passed to an `EventStore` hook (log
          +-only today), which the planned stats, costs and history featu
          +res will persist.                                             
       95 +                                                              
       96 +**Startup and resilience:**                                   
       97 +- The index loads in a background task, so the API is up immed
          +iately and reports progress in `/health`.                     
       98 +- Temporary failures, such as Ollama still starting, are retri
          +ed. Configuration errors fail fast with a clear message.      
       99 +- The index is written to a staging folder and swapped in, wit
          +h the manifest written last. A crash never leaves a half-writt
          +en index, and nothing is renamed across the Docker volume boun
          +dary.                                                         
      100 +- Logs are JSON lines with a request id (the `X-Request-ID` he
          +ader, passed through by nginx).                               
      101 +                                                              
      102 +---                                                           
      103 +                                                              
      104 +## API                                                        
      105 +                                                              
      106 +Base path `/api/v1`. The OpenAPI schema is generated by FastAP
          +I (`/docs` on the backend). Every error has the same shape:   
      107 +                                                              
      108 +```json                                                       
      109 +{ "error": { "code": "provider_unavailable", "message": "Ollam
          +a did not respond within 300 s ..." } }                       
      110 +```                                                           
      111 +                                                              
      112 +| Method & path | Purpose |                                   
      113 +|---|---|                                                     
      114 +| `POST /chat` | Answer a question and return the full result 
          +as JSON |                                                     
      115 +| `POST /chat/stream` | The same, as **Server-Sent Events**: `
          +meta` → `token`… → `done`, or `error` |                       
      116 +| `GET /health` | Liveness (always 200) and readiness: `status
          +` `starting`/`ok`/`degraded`, index stats, Ollama status |    
      117 +| `GET /providers` | The enabled LLM providers, their models, 
          +the default, and whether each is usable now |                 
      118 +                                                              
      119 +**Request** (`POST /chat` and `/chat/stream`):                
      120 +                                                              
      121 +```json                                                       
      122 +{                                                             
      123 +  "message": "How long are backups retained?",                
      124 +  "conversation_id": "optional-id",                           
      125 +  "options": { "provider": "anthropic" }                      
      126 +}                                                             
      127 +```                                                           
      128 +                                                              
      129 +- `message` must be 1–4,000 characters and not blank.         
      130 +- Unknown fields and options are **rejected** (422) instead of
          + silently ignored. New optional fields, such as the planned `l
          +anguage` and `detail` options, can be added without breaking c
          +lients.                                                       
      131 +                                                              
      132 +**Response** (shortened):                                     
      133 +                                                              
      134 +```json                                                       
      135 +{                                                             
      136 +  "conversation_id": "7f3c…",                                 
      137 +  "message_id": "a91e…",                                      
      138 +  "answer": "Backups are retained for **35 days** [1].",      
      139 +  "citations": [                                              
      140 +    { "number": 1, "doc_id": "kb-003", "title": "Data Retentio
          +n, Backup & GDPR Policy",                                     
      141 +      "section": "Backups and restore", "snippet": "Backup fre
          +quency: daily full snapshots…",                               
      142 +      "score": 0.6831, "chunk_id": "kb-003#004" }             
      143 +  ],                                                          
      144 +  "refused": false,                                           
      145 +  "refusal_reason": null,                                     
      146 +  "provider": "anthropic",                                    
      147 +  "model": "claude-opus-5",                                   
      148 +  "usage": { "input_tokens": 1138, "output_tokens": 93, "cache
          +_read_input_tokens": 0, "cache_creation_input_tokens": 0 },   
      149 +  "timings": { "embed_ms": 48.1, "search_ms": 0.2, "time_to_fi
          +rst_token_ms": 2103.5, "generation_ms": 2650.9, "total_ms": 27
          +01.4 },                                                       
      150 +  "top_score": 0.6831,                                        
      151 +  "sources_used": 4,                                          
      152 +  "stop_reason": "end_turn"                                   
      153 +}                                                             
      154 +```                                                           
      155 +                                                              
      156 +`refusal_reason` is one of:                                   
      157 +- `low_score`: nothing relevant was retrieved, so the LLM wasn
          +'t called                                                     
      158 +- `no_citations`: the model said the documentation doesn't cov
          +er the question                                               
      159 +- `model_refusal`: the provider declined the request          
      160 +                                                              
      161 +**Streaming events** (`/chat/stream`):                        
      162 +                                                              
      163 +```text                                                       
      164 +event: meta    data: {"conversation_id": "…", "message_id": "…
          +", "sources": [ …candidate citations… ]}                      
      165 +event: token   data: {"text": "Backups are "}                 
      166 +event: token   data: {"text": "retained for 35 days [1]."}    
      167 +event: done    data: { …the same object as POST /chat… }      
      168 +```                                                           
      169 +                                                              
      170 +Errors before streaming starts (validation, unknown provider, 
          +index still loading) are normal HTTP errors. Errors during str
          +eaming arrive as `event: error` with `{code, message}`.       
      171 +                                                              
      172 +| Status | `code` | When |                                    
      173 +|---|---|---|                                                 
      174 +| 400 | `invalid_provider` | Unknown or disabled provider |   
      175 +| 422 | `validation_error` | Invalid request body |           
      176 +| 502 | `provider_error` | The provider rejected the request |
      177 +| 503 | `not_ready`, `provider_unavailable`, `provider_not_con
          +figured` | Index still loading, provider down, overloaded or r
          +ate-limited, or API key missing |                             
      178 +                                                              
      179 +---                                                           
      180 +                                                              
      181 +## Key decisions and trade-offs                               
      182 +                                                              
      183 +The full research, with arguments for and against each option,
          + is in [`documentation/taskdocs/`](documentation/taskdocs/) (`
          +research.md`, `storage.md`, `ideas.md`, `steps.md`).          
      184 +                                                              
      185 +| Decision | Why | Trade-off / when to revisit |              
      186 +|---|---|---|                                                 
      187 +| **Python + FastAPI** | Best RAG and numeric ecosystem; Pydan
          +tic contracts and generated OpenAPI; native async streaming | 
          +Two languages in the repo (TypeScript frontend) |             
      188 +| **Raw SDKs, no LangChain/LlamaIndex** | The pipeline is abou
          +t 200 lines: every step is visible, testable and explainable; 
          +full control of the prompt, citations and refusals; fewer depe
          +ndencies | Features like query rewriting or re-ranking must be
          + written by hand |                                            
      189 +| **In-memory exact vector search + disk cache** | About 50 ch
          +unks: brute force is *exact* and takes microseconds (p95 15 ms
          + even at **50,000** chunks); no extra database container; the 
          +cache avoids re-embedding on restart | Single process, no meta
          +data filters or incremental updates. The `VectorStore` interfa
          +ce lets pgvector replace it past about 100k chunks or with sev
          +eral replicas |                                               
      190 +| **Heading-aware chunking** | Sections stay coherent, and the
          + heading path gives human-readable citations | Tuned for struc
          +tured Markdown articles, not arbitrary PDFs |                 
      191 +| **Similarity threshold before the LLM** | Off-topic question
          +s are refused in about 0.1 s at zero token cost | Can't separa
          +te *near-topic* unanswerable questions (they score like real o
          +nes), so the prompt handles those; calibration data is in `sto
          +rage.md` |                                                    
      192 +| **"No valid citation = refusal"** | Language-independent ref
          +usal detection, no second LLM call | Depends on the model foll
          +owing the citation rule; measured at 100% on the eval set |   
      193 +| **Provider registries (LLM and embeddings separate)** | Swit
          +ch models by configuration or per request; fakes in tests; Ant
          +hropic has no embeddings API, so embeddings are their own inte
          +rface | More indirection than hard-coding one model |         
      194 +| **`claude-opus-5` default for Claude, server-side refusal fa
          +llback, cached system prompt** | Anthropic's current recommend
          +ed default; a safety-filter refusal is retried on a fallback m
          +odel; prompt caching cuts input cost | Opus costs more than So
          +nnet or Haiku: change `ANTHROPIC_MODEL` to trade quality for c
          +ost |                                                         
      195 +| **`gemma3:4b` + `embeddinggemma` for local use** | The best 
          +quality that runs on a CPU in about 6 GB of RAM; both are mult
          +ilingual (German questions work over English documents) | Abou
          +t 5 tokens/s on a laptop CPU, so answers take about a minute (
          +see below) |                                                  
      196 +| **SSE over POST (not WebSockets)** | One-directional token s
          +tream, works through nginx and plain HTTP, easy to test | `Eve
          +ntSource` only supports GET, so the frontend parses the stream
          + itself (`src/api/sse.ts`) |                                  
      197 +| **Background index loading** | The container is healthy at o
          +nce; a slow first build doesn't block health checks | Clients 
          +must handle `503 not_ready` (the UI does) |                   
      198 +| **nginx serves the UI and forwards `/api`** | Same origin, s
          +o no CORS; security headers and CSP; gzip; SSE without bufferi
          +ng | One more container |                                     
      199 +| **Future features as seams, not code** | Stats, costs, histo
          +ry, languages and the admin tabs are planned (`ideas.md`); `Ch
          +atResult`, `EventStore`, `options` and the registries already 
          +exist for them | Some structure exists before its feature does
          + |                                                            
      200 +                                                              
      201 +---                                                           
      202 +                                                              
      203 +## Testing and evaluation                                     
      204 +                                                              
      205 +Details and raw numbers: **[`documentation/evaluation.md`](doc
          +umentation/evaluation.md)**.                                  
      206 +                                                              
      207 +```bash                                                       
      208 +cd backend                                                    
      209 +uv run pytest                    # 101 unit + API tests, offli
          +ne (fakes, mock HTTP), 95% coverage with --cov                
      210 +uv run pytest -m perf -s         # speed tests: vector search 
          +to 50k chunks, API throughput                                 
      211 +uv run pytest -m "integration or eval" -s                 # ne
          +eds Ollama (docker compose up -d ollama)                      
      212 +uv run python -m app.evaluation.answers --provider anthropic -
          +-show    # 18-question benchmark (~$0.20)                     
      213 +uv run python -m app.evaluation.answers --provider ollama     
          +         # the same, locally                                  
      214 +cd ../frontend && npm test       # 14 Vitest + Testing Library
          + tests                                                        
      215 +```                                                           
      216 +                                                              
      217 +CI (`.github/workflows/ci.yml`) runs the offline suites on eve
          +ry push: backend lint, types, tests, coverage and perf; fronte
          +nd lint, types, tests and build; and a Docker image build.    
      218 +                                                              
      219 +**Evaluation set:** 18 questions (`backend/tests/eval/question
          +s.yaml`):                                                     
      220 +- 10 answered by a single document                            
      221 +- 4 that need two documents                                   
      222 +- 1 in German                                                 
      223 +- 3 that the knowledge base **can't** answer                  
      224 +                                                              
      225 +An answerable question passes when it is answered, cites an ex
          +pected document **and** contains every key fact. An unanswerab
          +le one passes when it is refused.                             
      226 +                                                              
      227 +| | Claude `claude-opus-5` | Local `gemma3:4b` (4-core i5 lapt
          +op CPU) |                                                     
      228 +|---|---|---|                                                 
      229 +| Passed | **18 / 18** | OLLAMA_PASSED |                      
      230 +| Refusals correct | 3 / 3 | OLLAMA_REFUSALS |                
      231 +| Time to first token (p50) | 2.6 s | OLLAMA_TTFT |           
      232 +| Full answer (p50 / p95) | 4.8 s / 11.5 s | OLLAMA_TOTAL |   
      233 +| Cost per question | about $0.011 | $0 |                     
      234 +                                                              
      235 +**Retrieval** (`embeddinggemma`, top 6):                      
      236 +- recall of the expected documents: **0.97**                  
      237 +- at least one expected document for every question           
      238 +- query embedding: about 50 ms                                
      239 +                                                              
      240 +**Without the model:** the API handles about 450 requests/s (p
          +95 54 ms). The index builds in 16 s and loads from cache in 5 
          +ms.                                                           
      241 +                                                              
      242 +---                                                           
      243 +                                                              
      244 +## Configuration                                              
      245 +                                                              
      246 +All settings are environment variables, read from `.env` (see 
          +[`.env.example`](.env.example) for every option with comments)
          +. Only non-secret defaults are committed; `.env` is git-ignore
          +d.                                                            
      247 +                                                              
      248 +| Variable | Default | Meaning |                              
      249 +|---|---|---|                                                 
      250 +| `LLM_PROVIDER` | `ollama` | Default chat provider: `ollama` 
          +or `anthropic` |                                              
      251 +| `ENABLED_LLM_PROVIDERS` | `ollama,anthropic` | Providers tha
          +t may be used; others are rejected |                          
      252 +| `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` | – | Your key (bring your own) |       
      253 +| `ANTHROPIC_MODEL` | `claude-opus-5` | e.g. `claude-sonnet-5`
          + or `claude-haiku-4-5` for lower cost |                       
      254 +| `ANTHROPIC_EFFORT` | – | Optional: `low`/`medium` for faster
          +, cheaper answers |                                           
      255 +| `OLLAMA_CHAT_MODEL` / `OLLAMA_EMBED_MODEL` | `gemma3:4b` / `
          +embeddinggemma` | Local models, pulled automatically |        
      256 +| `OLLAMA_TIMEOUT_S` | `300` | Maximum wait for the first toke
          +n |                                                           
      257 +| `TOP_K` / `MIN_SCORE` | `6` / `0.35` | Chunks sent to the LL
          +M; refusal threshold (calibrated) |                           
      258 +| `CHUNK_MAX_WORDS` / `CHUNK_OVERLAP_WORDS` | `300` / `45` | C
          +hunk size; changing them rebuilds the index |                 
      259 +| `FRONTEND_PORT` | `8080` | Web UI port |                    
      260 +

      290 +                                                              
      291 +## Known limitations and future work                          
      292 +                                                              
      293 +- **Single-turn questions.** Each question is answered on its 
          +own: `conversation_id` is returned and reused, but earlier tur
          +ns aren't used as context, so a follow-up like *"and on the En
          +terprise plan?"* loses the topic. Multi-turn context (question
          + rewriting over the history) is part of the planned response-h
          +istory feature (`ideas.md` #5).                               
      294 +- **No authentication or rate limiting.** Fine for a local pro
          +totype. Before exposing it (especially with a Claude key), add
          + auth such as SSO or an API gateway and per-user rate limits. 
      295 +- **Ollama is always required** for embeddings, even when Clau
          +de answers. Anthropic has no embeddings API. A "Claude only" m
          +ode needs an in-process or Voyage embedding provider (`ideas.m
          +d` #7; the interface exists).                                 
      296 +- **Local answers are slow on a CPU** (about 5 tokens/s). A GP
          +U or Claude is recommended for interactive use.               
      297 +- **Small knowledge base and evaluation set** (5 articles, 18 
          +questions). Enough to validate the design, not to tune it stat
          +istically.                                                    
      298 +- **English knowledge base.** Questions in other languages wor
          +k, and the answer comes in the question's language; there's no
          + language selector yet (`ideas.md` #4).                       
      299 +- **Planned** (with the seams already in the code, see [`ideas
          +.md`](documentation/taskdocs/ideas.md)): hidden statistics, co
          +sts, history and tests tabs; concise vs detailed answers; a mo
          +del on/off toggle.                                            
      300 +                                                              
      301 +---

      319  Customer Success Managers (CSMs) at OmniCorp Solutions current
           ly spend hours manually searching through hundreds of internal
            product manuals to answer complex client configuration questi
           ons. You have been contracted to build a prototype Retrieval-A
           ugmented Generation (RAG) chatbot that allows CSMs to ask natu
           ral language questions and receive accurate, cited answers bas
           ed strictly on internal documentation.
      320  
      321  Your task is to build a full-stack application consisting of a
            backend API and a frontend chat interface. The backend should
            ingest a small set of provided text documents (you should cre
           ate 3-5 mock enterprise knowledge base articles), chunk them, 
           store them in a local or in-memory vector store, and expose a 
           chat endpoint. The frontend must be a web-based UI where users
            can type questions, view the AI's response, and critically, s
           ee the specific document citations used to generate the answer
           .

      295  - **Single-turn questions.** Each question is answered on its 
           own: `conversation_id` is returned and reused, but earlier tur
           ns aren't used as context, so a follow-up like *"and on the En
           terprise plan?"* loses the topic. Multi-turn context (question
            rewriting over the history) is part of the planned response-h
           istory feature (`ideas.md` #5).
      296  - **No authentication or rate limiting.** Fine for a local pro
           totype. Before exposing it (especially with a Claude key), add
            auth such as SSO or an API gateway and per-user rate limits.
      297  - **Ollama is always required** for embeddings, even when Clau
           de answers. Anthropic has no embeddings API. A "Claude only" m
           ode needs an in-process or Voyage embedding provider (`ideas.m
           d` #7; the interface exists).
      296 -- **Local answers are slow on a CPU** (about 5 tokens/s). A GP

  What got done, in the order I suggested:

  1. Pushed the review fixes (eff0390): the nginx timeout and the stale docs.
  2. Re-measured the local model on the host CPU. The VM now has an Intel
     i5-8300H with AVX2.

  |                          | Before (emulated CPU) | Now         |
  |--------------------------|-----------------------|-------------|
  | Embedding a question     | ~1 s                  | ~50 ms      |
  | Building the index       | ~5 min                | 16 s        |
  | Off-topic refusal        | 0.8 s                 | ~50 ms      |
  | Local answer (gemma3:4b) | timed out             | ~80 s (p50) |
     On the 18-question evaluation set, the local model passes 14/18, against
     18/18 for Claude:
     - Three of the misses are correct answers that leave out one detail.
     - The fourth is an unsupported claim: it said OmniCorp "does not integrate
       with LDAP", which the documentation doesn't say. Claude correctly refused
       that question.
     - That's why the README recommends Claude for quality and speed, and the
       local model for privacy or offline use.

     for:
     - quick start
     - architecture, with a Mermaid diagram
     - the API, with request/response examples and error codes
     - decisions and trade-offs
     - testing and results (Claude and the local model side by side)

  and CPU:

  - qwen3.5:4b: its two misses (q11, q13) each leave out one of two key facts.
    It is about 50% slower than gemma3:4b, around 2 minutes per answer.
  - ministral-3:3b: it refused all 3 unanswerable questions, and each of its 3
    misses (q06, q10, q11) is an answer that leaves out a detail.
    - It has an Apache-2.0 license.
    - It answered the German question in German, and so did qwen3.5:4b.
  - gemma3:4b is the only model that made an unsupported claim (the LDAP
    answer).
  - The other three refused correctly but missed too many facts to be useful.

  My recommendation: make ministral-3:3b the default local model. At about 1
  minute per answer it is the most usable of the stronger models on this CPU.
  I'd document qwen3.5:4b as the option when answer quality matters more than
  speed. It needs OLLAMA_THINK=false, otherwise it spends minutes reasoning
  first. Claude stays the recommendation for demos (18/18, about 5 s).

  The benchmark results are in the scratchpad for now; nothing in the repo has
  changed yet. Should I switch the default to ministral-3:3b, or keep gemma3:4b?

  Switching means updating:
  - config.py, docker-compose.yml and .env.example
  - evaluation.md, research.md and the README
  - the result files in documentation/eval/

  Then I'd rebuild, check the stack runs, and push to PR #2. Your .env sets
  OLLAMA_CHAT_MODEL=gemma3:4b, so you'd need to change that line yourself.

  - Ollama model library (https://ollama.com/search?o=newest)
